======================================================================
ASTRONOMY KNOWLEDGE BASE
======================================================================

Entry 1
Category: Astronomy Fundamentals
Concept: Astronomy
Definition: The scientific study of celestial objects, space and the universe.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in astronomy.
- Important in astrophysics and space science.
- Frequently covered in educational and competitive examinations.
Example:
Astronomy is an important topic in astronomy.
Related Concepts:
Stars, Galaxies, Cosmology, Space Exploration, Gravity, Celestial Mechanics

Entry 5
Category: Astronomy Fundamentals
Concept: Solar System
Definition: The Sun and all objects gravitationally bound to it.

- Fundamental concept in astronomy.
- Important in astrophysics and space science.
- Frequently covered in educational and competitive examinations.
Example:
The Solar System has eight recognised planets.

- Frequently covered in educational and competitive examinations.
Example:
Dark Matter is an important topic in astronomy.

Entry 34
Category: Cosmology & Physics
Concept: Light Year
Definition: The distance light travels in one year.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in astronomy.
- Important in astrophysics and space science.
- Frequently covered in educational and competitive examinations.
Example:
One light year is approximately 9.46 trillion kilometres.
Related Concepts:

Entry 35
Category: Cosmology & Physics
Concept: Astronomical Unit
Definition: The average distance between Earth and the Sun.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in astronomy.
- Important in astrophysics and space science.
- Frequently covered in educational and competitive examinations.
Example:
1 AU is approximately 149.6 million kilometres.
Related Concepts:
Stars, Galaxies, Cosmology, Space Exploration, Gravity, Celestial Mechanics

Category: Celestial Objects
Concept: Meteorite
